\displaystyle\frac{2\left\|V\right\|}{\beta}\left[4\mathfrak{g}_{1}+\mathfrak{g}_{2}+\frac{6}{\kappa_{0}}\left(\mathfrak{g}_{3}\left\|V\right\|+\frac{\mathfrak{g}_{4}}{9g^{\prime}_{\tau}}\right)\right]\leq\left\|V\right\|\left[\Theta(\beta^{D})\log(\beta/\delta t))+\frac{\left\|V\right\|}{\kappa_{0}}\Theta(\beta^{D+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left\|V\right\|\left[\Theta(\beta^{D})\log(r+\tau+e)+\Theta(\beta^{D})\log(\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\frac{\left\|V\right\|}{\kappa_{0}}\Theta(\beta^{D+1})\right].

(S.353)

By applying the inequalities (
S.353
) to (
S.351
), we have to satisfy the inequalities of

\displaystyle\kappa_{0}\geq\left\|V\right\|\left[\Theta(\beta^{D})\log(\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\frac{\left\|V\right\|}{\kappa_{0}}\Theta(\beta^{D+1})\log(\beta)\right]

(S.354)

and

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}\log(r+\tau+e)\geq\left\|V\right\|\Theta(\beta^{D})\log(r+\tau+e).

(S.355)

The above condition is satisfied by choosing

\displaystyle\kappa_{0}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|),\quad\kappa_{1}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|.

(S.356)

This completes the proof of Subtheorem
1
.
\square

S.V.2
Proof of Proposition
23

We start from upper-bounding the integrals of
g_{1}(t)
[or
g_{2}(t)
] and
|t|g_{1}(t)
[or
|t|g_{2}(t)
].
We can prove the following lemma:

Lemma 26
.

For an arbitrary
\delta t>0
such that

\displaystyle\delta t\leq\frac{\beta}{4\pi},

(S.357)

the integrals of
\{g_{1}(t),|t|g_{1}(t)\}
and
\{g_{2}(t),|t|g_{2}(t)\}
are upper-bounded by

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{1}(t)dt=\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{2\beta}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right),

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|t|^{m}g_{1}(t)dt=\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq 2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}m!\zeta(m+1)\quad(m\geq 1).

(S.358)

Also, for an arbitrary
\delta t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|t|^{m}g_{2}(t)dt=\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|t^{m+1}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta}{(m+1)\pi}\delta t^{m+1}\quad(m\geq 0),

(S.359)

where
\zeta(x)
is the Riemann zeta function, i.e.,
\zeta(x)=\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}(1/s^{x})
, which is given by
\zeta(2)=\pi^{2}x/6
,
\zeta(3)=1.20205\cdots
,
\zeta(4)=\pi^{4}/90
, and so on.

Proof of Lemma
26
.

From the definition (
S.92
), i.e.,

g_{\beta}(t)=-{\rm sign}(t)\frac{e^{-2\pi|t|/\beta}}{1-e^{-2\pi|t|/\beta}},

the function
g_{\beta}(t)
is an odd function,
g_{\beta}(-t)=-g_{\beta}(t)
.
Thus, from
|g_{\beta}(-t)|=|g_{\beta}(t)|
, we obtain by letting
z=2\pi t/\beta
and
\delta z=2\pi\delta t/\beta
(
\leq 1/2
)

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle=2\int_{\delta t}^{\infty}|g_{\beta}(t)|dt=2\int_{\delta z}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-z}}{1-e^{-z}}\frac{\beta dz}{2\pi}=\frac{\beta}{\pi}\int_{\delta z}^{\infty}\frac{1}{e^{z}-1}dz

\displaystyle=-\frac{\beta}{\pi}\log\left(1-e^{-\delta z}\right)\leq\frac{\beta}{\pi}\left[\delta z+\log(1/\delta z)\right],

(S.360)

and

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle=2\int_{\delta t}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\beta z}{2\pi}\right)^{m}\cdot\frac{e^{-z}}{1-e^{-z}}\frac{\beta dz}{2\pi}=2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\int_{\delta z}^{\infty}\frac{z^{m}}{e^{z}-1}dz

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{m}}{e^{z}-1}dz=2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}m!\zeta(m+1),

(S.361)

where
\zeta(x)
has been defined as the Riemann zeta function.
By substituting
\delta z
by
2\pi\delta t/\beta
, we prove the first inequality in (
S.358
), where we use the inequalities of
x+\log(1/x)\leq 2\log(1/x)
for
x\leq 1/2
.

In the same way, we can derive

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|t^{m+1}g_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle=2\int_{0}^{\delta z}\left(\frac{\beta z}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\frac{e^{-z}}{1-e^{-z}}\frac{\beta dz}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+2}\int_{0}^{\delta z}\frac{z^{m+1}}{e^{z}-1}dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{m+1}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+2}\delta z^{m+1}=\frac{\beta}{(m+1)\pi}\delta t^{m+1},

(S.362)

where we use
\int_{0}^{z_{0}}\frac{z^{m}}{e^{z}-1}\leq z_{0}^{m}/m
for arbitrary
z_{0}>0
and
m\in\mathbb{N}
and substitute
\delta z
by
2\pi\delta t/\beta
in the last equation.
We thus prove the second main inequality (
S.359
).
This completes the proof.
\square

[ End of Proof of Lemma
26
]

We then rely on Lemma
26
for the proof.
We start from the case of
f(t)=g_{1}(t)
in the statement to derive the first inequality in (
S.324
).
Using the inequality (
S.358
) in Lemma
26
, the parameters
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
in (
S.59
) immediately given by

\displaystyle f_{1}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{1}(t)dt\leq\frac{2\beta}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right),

\displaystyle f_{2}\leq\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|t|g_{1}(t)dt\leq\frac{\beta^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\zeta(2)=\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}.

(S.363)

Also, because
t_{0}=\max(0,\mu s\Delta\ell/(2v)-\mu(\Delta\ell-1)/v)=[\mu/(2v)]\Delta\ell(s-2)+\mu/v
for
s\geq 2
from the definition, we have

\displaystyle f_{t_{0}}(s)=\int_{|t|\geq t_{0}}g_{1}(t)dt

\displaystyle=-\frac{\beta}{\pi}\log\left(1-e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}\right)\leq\frac{\beta}{\pi}\log\left(e+\frac{e\beta}{2\pi t_{0}}\right)e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta}{\pi}\log\left(e+\frac{e}{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\right)e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell}\leq\frac{2\beta}{\pi}e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell}

(S.364)

for
s\geq 2
,
where we use Eq. (
S.360
), the inequality
\log(1-e^{-x})\leq\log(e+e/x)e^{-x}
,

2\pi t_{0}/\beta\geq\pi\mu(s-2)\Delta\ell/(v\beta)\geq 2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell
, and

\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell\geq 1
from the condition (
S.326
).
Recall that
2\kappa_{\beta}\leq\min\left(\frac{\pi\mu}{v\beta},\frac{\mu}{2},2\right)\leq\frac{\pi\mu}{v\beta}
from the definition (
S.121
).
For
s\leq 1
, we can adopt
f_{t_{0}}(s)\leq f_{1}
by letting
t_{0}=0
.
Note that
\log(e+e/x)e^{-x}
monotonically decreases with
x
for
x\geq 0
.

Using the above estimations, we calculate the summation in the RHS of (
S.58
) from the choice of
\mathcal{F}(\ell)
as in Eq. (
S.322
).
First, we obtain the following upper bound from the inequalities (
S.363
) and
\kappa_{\beta}\leq\mu/4
from the definition of
\kappa_{\beta}
:

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma Cv}{6}\beta^{2}(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}e^{K_{\ell-s\Delta\ell}-\kappa_{\beta}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)}e^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma Cv}{6}\beta^{2}(\Delta\ell)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell)\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}e^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma Cv}{6}\beta^{2}(\Delta\ell)^{D}e^{-\mu\Delta\ell/4}\left(1+2^{D}D!\left[\max\left(1,\frac{4}{\mu\Delta\ell}\right)\right]^{D}\right)\mathcal{F}(\ell)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma Cv\beta^{2}}{6\mu^{D}}(\mu\Delta\ell)^{D}e^{-\mu\Delta\ell/4}\left(1+2^{D}D!\right)\mathcal{F}(\ell)\leq\frac{\gamma Cv\beta^{2}}{3}\left(\frac{8D^{2}}{e\mu}\right)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell),

(S.365)

where we use
K_{\ell^{\prime}}\leq K_{\ell}
for
\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell
in the second inequality,
[
166
, (S.11) of Lemma 1 therein]
in the third inequality,

4/(\mu\Delta\ell)\leq 1
in the fourth inequality, which originates from
\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell\geq 1
and
\kappa_{\beta}=\min\left(\frac{\pi\mu}{2v\beta},\frac{\mu}{4}\right)
from (
S.326
), and
(\mu\Delta\ell)^{D}e^{-\mu\Delta\ell/4}\leq(4D/e)^{D}
from
\mu\Delta\ell\geq 4
in the last inequality.
In the same way, we secondly obtain using the inequality (
S.364
)

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)f_{t_{0}}(s)

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}\mathcal{F}(\ell-\Delta\ell)+\frac{4\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}\sum_{s=2}^{\infty}s^{D}e^{K_{\ell-s\Delta\ell}-\kappa_{\beta}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)}e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{4\gamma(2\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}\mathcal{F}(\ell)e^{3\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\sum_{\tilde{s}=1}^{\infty}\tilde{s}^{D}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}\tilde{s}\Delta\ell}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{4\gamma(2\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\left(1+2^{D}D!\left[\max\left(1,\frac{1}{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\right)\right]^{D}\right)\mathcal{F}(\ell)

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{4\gamma(2\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\left(1+2^{D}D!\right)\mathcal{F}(\ell)

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{8\gamma(4D\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell),

(S.366)

where we let
s=\tilde{s}+1
[
(\tilde{s}+1)^{D}\leq(2\tilde{s})^{D}
] and use the condition
\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell\geq 1
.
Therefore, by combining the inequalities (
S.365
) and (
S.366
), we obtain

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)\left[Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}f_{1}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{8\gamma(4D\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta}{\pi}e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+\frac{\gamma Cv\beta^{2}}{3}\left(\frac{8D^{2}}{e\mu}\right)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell).

(S.367)

By applying the inequalities (
S.363
) and (
S.367
) to (
S.58
), we obtain the first main inequality in (
S.324
) with the choice of
\mathfrak{g}_{1}
as in Eq. (
S.325
) in the case of
f(t)=g_{1}(t)
.

We next consider the case of
f(t)=g_{2}(t)
to derive the second inequality in (
S.324
).
In this case, we simply choose
\Delta\ell=1
in utilizing the inequality (
S.60
) in Lemma
5
.
Because of
\delta t<t_{0}=\mu s/(2v)
, we have
f_{t_{0}}(s)=0
from the definition of
g_{2}(t)
, where
g_{2}(t)=0
for
t\leq\delta t
as in Eq. (
S.323
).
The inequality (
S.359
) in Lemma
26
gives the parameters
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle f_{1}\leq\frac{\beta\delta t}{\pi},\quad f_{2}\leq\frac{\beta\delta t^{2}}{2\pi}.

(S.368)

Then, we upper-bound
Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)
by

\displaystyle Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\leq\frac{Cv\beta\delta t^{2}}{2\pi}e^{-\mu s/2}.

(S.369)

By using the same calculations as (
S.365
), we obtain

\displaystyle 2\gamma\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s)\left[Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma Cv\beta\delta t^{2}}{\pi}\cdot 2^{D+1}D!(1+4/\mu)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell)

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta\delta t}{\pi}\cdot 2^{D+1}D!\gamma Cv(1+4/\mu)^{D}\delta t\mathcal{F}(\ell),

(S.370)

where we use
\max\left(1,4/\mu\right)\leq(1+4/\mu)
.
We therefore obtain the second inequality in (
S.324
) with the choice of
\mathfrak{g}_{2}
as in Eq. (
S.325
) by applying the inequalities (
S.368
) and
(
S.370
) to (
S.60
).
This completes the proof of Proposition
23
.
\square

S.V.3
Proof of Proposition
24

S.V.3.1
Preliminary lemmas

We first prove several supplemental lemmas to prove the main statement.
First of all, we prove the following lemma:

Lemma 27
.

For an arbitrary positive
\nu
, the condition

\displaystyle r^{\nu}\check{Q}(r,\tau)\leq e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r/2}

(S.371)

is satisfied with

\displaystyle r\geq\frac{\tau+e}{2}+\frac{32}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}^{2}}\left(\nu^{2}+\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau}{8}\right),

(S.372)

where
\check{Q}(r,\tau)
was defined in Eq. (
S.332
).

S.V.3.2
Proof of Lemma
27

We start with the expression (
S.332
) of

\displaystyle\check{Q}(\tau,r)=e^{\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r}.

(S.373)

Then, the condition (
S.371
) reads

\displaystyle\nu\log(r)+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)\leq\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r}{2}.

(S.374)

For arbitrary positive
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\nu\log(r)+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)

\displaystyle=\nu\log(c_{1}r)+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log[c_{2}(r+\tau+e)]-\nu\log(c_{1})-\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(c_{2})

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}\nu+c_{2}\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau)r+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau c_{2}(\tau+e)+\frac{\nu}{c_{1}}+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau}{c_{2}},

(S.375)

where we use
\log(x)\leq x
for
x\geq 0
.
By choosing
c_{1}=\check{\kappa}_{\beta}/(8\nu)
and
c_{2}=\check{\kappa}_{\beta}/(8\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau)
, we reduce the above inequality to

\displaystyle\nu\log(r)+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r}{4}+\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}{8}(\tau+e)+\frac{8\nu^{2}}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}+\frac{8\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}.

(S.376)

Therefore, the inequality (
S.374
) reduces to

\displaystyle r\geq\frac{4}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}\left[\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}{8}(\tau+e)+\frac{8\nu^{2}}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}+\frac{8\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}\right]=\frac{\tau+e}{2}+\frac{32}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}^{2}}\left(\nu^{2}+\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau}{8}\right).

(S.377)

This completes the proof.
\square

[ End of Proof of Lemma
27
]

Based on Lemma
27
, we further prove the following statement:

Lemma 28
.

Under the definition of
\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)
in Eq. (
S.404
) as

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}(\tau,r):=\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s),

(S.378)

the
\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)
is upper-bounded as

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 2^{2D+2}\left(\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{2D}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r),\quad\check{C}_{\nu,\tau}:=\frac{\tau+e}{2}+\frac{32}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}^{2}}\left(\nu^{2}D^{2}+\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau}{8}\right),

(S.379)

where we have define
\bar{J}_{x}
in Eq. (
S.7
), i.e.,
\bar{J}_{x}:=\bar{J}_{0}e^{-\mu x}
.

Proof of Lemma
28
.

We begin with the estimation for the summation of
\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)
, which is upper-bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\leq r^{2D-1}\sum_{s<r}\bar{J}_{s}\check{Q}(\tau,r-s)\leq r^{2D-1}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\sum_{s<r}\bar{J}_{s}e^{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}s},

(S.380)

where we use
\check{Q}(\tau,r-s)\leq\check{Q}(\tau,r)e^{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}s}
from the form of
\check{Q}(\tau,r)
in Eq. (
S.332
).
Because of
\bar{J}_{s}=\bar{J}_{0}e^{-\mu s}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s<r}\bar{J}_{s}e^{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}s}=\bar{J}_{0}\sum_{s<r}e^{-(\mu-\check{\kappa}_{\beta})s}\leq\frac{\bar{J}_{0}}{1-e^{-\mu+\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}}\leq\bar{J}_{0}\left(1+\frac{1}{\mu-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}\right)\leq\bar{J}_{0}\left(1+\frac{1}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}\right),

(S.381)

which reduces the inequality (
S.380
) to

\displaystyle\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\leq\bar{J}_{0}\left(1+\frac{1}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}}\right)r^{2D-1}\check{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.382)

where we use
1/(1-e^{-x})\leq 1+1/x
and
\mu-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\geq 2\kappa_{\beta}-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\geq\check{\kappa}_{\beta}
from
\kappa_{\beta}\geq\check{\kappa}_{\beta}
from the condition (
S.332
).

We next estimate an upper bound for
\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)
.
For this purpose, we first define
s_{0}
such that for
s\geq s_{0}

\displaystyle s^{2D-1}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\leq e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}s/2}.

(S.383)

Here, using Lemma
27
with
\nu=2D-1
, we can ensure

\displaystyle s_{0}\leq\frac{\tau+e}{2}+\frac{32}{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}^{2}}\left[(2D-1)^{2}+\check{\kappa}_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}+\frac{\check{\kappa}_{\beta}\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau}{8}\right]\leq\check{C}_{2,\tau},

(S.384)

where we use the definition in Eq. (
S.379
).
Then, for
r\geq s_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\leq s<2r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq 2r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)

\displaystyle\leq(2r)^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)+\sum_{s\geq 2r}\bar{J}_{0}e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}s/2}

\displaystyle\leq(2r)^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)+\frac{\bar{J}_{0}e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r}}{1-e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}/2}}\leq 2(2r)^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.385)

On the other hand, for
r<s_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\leq s<s_{0}}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq s_{0}}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)

\displaystyle\leq s_{0}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)+2(2s_{0})^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s_{0})\leq 3(2s_{0})^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.386)

where we apply the upper bound in (
S.385
) with
r=s_{0}
to the summation of
\sum_{s\geq s_{0}}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)
.
Therefore, the following upper bound holds for general
r
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\leq 3\cdot 2^{2D}(s_{0}^{2D}+r^{2D})\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.387)

Combining the inequalities (
S.382
) and (
S.387
) and using
s_{0}\leq\check{C}_{2,\tau}
from (
S.384
), we prove the main inequality (
S.379
).
This completes the proof.
\square

[ End of Proof of Lemma
28
]

Lemma 29
.

Using Lemma
28
, we can obtain the following upper bound:

\displaystyle\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 2^{2D+5}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0},

(S.388)

where
\check{C}_{3,\tau}
is defined by (
S.379
).

Proof of Lemma
29
.

We begin with the upper bound of (
S.379
) as

\displaystyle r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2D+2}r^{D-1}\left(\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{2D}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2D+2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}r^{D-1}\left(1+\frac{r^{2D}}{\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2D+2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}r^{D-1}\left(2r^{2D}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 2^{2D+3}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}r^{3D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.389)

Then, we introduce the parameter
r_{0}
such that for
r\geq r_{0}

\displaystyle r^{3D-1}Q(\tau,r)\leq e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r/2}.

(S.390)

Using the notation of
r_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r\leq r_{0}}2^{2D+3}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}r^{3D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)+\sum_{r>r_{0}}2^{2D+3}C_{2,\tau}^{2D}r^{3D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2D+3}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\left(2r_{0}^{3D}+\sum_{r>r_{0}}e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r/2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2D+3}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\left(2r_{0}^{3D}+\frac{e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}r_{0}/2}}{1-e^{-\check{\kappa}_{\beta}/2}}\right)\leq 2^{2D+5}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}r_{0}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0},

(S.391)

where we use
Q(\tau,r)\leq 2
as a trivial upper bound [see (
S.302
)].
Finally, from Lemma
27
, we can choose
r_{0}=\check{C}_{3,\tau}
to satifsy the condition (
S.390
), which reduces the above inequality to the main inequality (
S.388
).
This completes the proof.
\square

[ End of Proof of Lemma
29
]

S.V.3.3
Completing the proof of Proposition
24

Throughout the proof, we omit the
\min(2,\cdots)
notation from
\check{Q}(\tau,r)
in Eq. (
S.332
).
We begin with the equation of

\displaystyle{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(O)=[U_{\tau}(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau})U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]

\displaystyle=[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]+[U_{\tau}H_{0}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]

\displaystyle=[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]+[H_{0},O]+[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O].

(S.392)

We then consider
\left\|[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(O),u_{i}]\right\|
, which is upper-bounded by the sum of

\displaystyle\left\|\left[[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|,\quad\left\|\left[[H_{0},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|,\and\left\|\left[[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|.

(S.393)

For the first term in (
S.393
), we have from Eq. (
S.295
)

\displaystyle U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}=2\int_{0}^{\tau}U_{\tau}U_{\tau_{1}}^{\dagger}VU_{\tau_{1}}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}d\tau_{1},

(S.394)

which reduces
\left\|\left[[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|
to

\displaystyle\left\|2\int_{0}^{\tau}\left[\left[U_{\tau}U_{\tau_{1}}^{\dagger}VU_{\tau}^{\dagger}U_{\tau_{1}},O\right],u_{i}\right]\right\|d\tau_{1}\leq 4\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(2\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|\left[U_{\tau}U_{\tau_{1}}^{\dagger},u_{i}\right]\right\|+\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|[O,u_{i}]\right\|+\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|[V,u_{i}]\right\|\right)d\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 8\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|O\right\|\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\left\|[U_{\tau},u_{i}]\right\|+\left\|[U_{\tau_{1}},u_{i}]\right\|\right)d\tau_{1}+4\left\|V\right\|\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|[O,u_{i}]\right\|d\tau_{1}+4\left\|O\right\|\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|[V,u_{i}]\right\|d\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 8\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{N}_{O}\tau\left(2Q(\tau,r)+\frac{\check{Q}(\tau,r)+Q(0,r)}{2}\right)\leq 24\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{N}_{O}\tau\check{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.395)

where we use
\left\|O\right\|\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}
,
Q(\tau^{\prime},r)\leq Q(\tau,r)
for
\tau^{\prime}\leq\tau
, and Eq. (
S.296
) for
\left\|[V,u_{i}]\right\|
.
We thus obtain

\displaystyle\mbox{{\bf[{1st term in \eqref{0_three_term_H_tau_V_ad}}]}}=\left\|\left[[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|\leq 24\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{N}_{O}\tau\check{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.396)

For the second term in (
S.393
), we decompose
O
as

\displaystyle O=\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}}+\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\left(\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right),\quad\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}:=\tilde{{\rm tr}}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]^{\rm c}}(O).

(S.397)

Note that by using the inequality (
S.50
) in the proof of Lemma
3
,
Eq. (
S.331
) gives

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right\|\leq\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[s]\setminus\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\left\|[O,u_{i}]\right\|\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\check{Q}(\tau,s).

(S.398)

Then, for
i\notin\mathfrak{L}[s]
(or
d_{i,\mathfrak{L}}=r>s
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left[\left[H_{0},\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right],u_{i}\right]=\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}[s]\neq\emptyset,\ Z\cap\{i\}\neq\emptyset}\left[\left[h_{Z},\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right],u_{i}\right],

(S.399)

and hence, by using Lemma
1
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\left[H_{0},\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right],u_{i}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}[s]\neq\emptyset,\ Z\cap\{i\}\neq\emptyset}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\cdot\left\|\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s).

(S.400)

For
i\in\mathfrak{L}[s]
(or
d_{i,\mathfrak{L}}=r\leq s
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\left[H_{0},\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right],u_{i}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}[s]\neq\emptyset}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\cdot\left\|\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right\|\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s).

(S.401)

By using the inequality (
S.400
) and (
S.401
), we take the summation as

\displaystyle\left\|\left[[H_{0},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\sum_{s<r}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+4\mathcal{N}_{O}\sum_{s\geq r}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s),

(S.402)

where we let
\bar{J}_{x}=\bar{J}_{0}
for
x<0
.

For the summation with respect to
s
, we have

\displaystyle 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\sum_{s<r}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+4\mathcal{N}_{O}\sum_{s\geq r}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|\cdot|\partial\mathfrak{L}|\left(\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\left(\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\right),

(S.403)

where we use
|\partial\mathfrak{L}|\leq|\mathfrak{L}|
in the last inequality.
Then, by defining

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}(\tau,r):=\sum_{s<r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)+\sum_{s\geq r}s^{2D-1}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s),

(S.404)

we obtain

\displaystyle\mbox{{\bf[{2nd term in \eqref{0_three_term_H_tau_V_ad}}]}}=\left\|\left[[H_{0},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.405)

Finally, for the third term in (
S.393
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left[[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|\left(\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|[U_{\tau}^{\dagger},u_{i}]\right\|+\left\|[O,u_{i}]\right\|\right)+2\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|\cdot\left[\left\|O\right\|Q(\tau,r)+\mathcal{N}_{O}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\right]+2\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|\check{Q}(\tau,r)+2\mathcal{N}_{O}\left\|[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]\right\|,

(S.406)

where we use
\left\|[O,u_{i}]\right\|\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}\check{Q}(\tau,r)
(
\left\|O\right\|\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}
) and
\left\|[U_{\tau}^{\dagger},u_{i}]\right\|\leq Q(\tau,r)\leq\check{Q}(\tau,r)
from the assumption (
S.332
).
By using a similar calculation to (
S.398
), we obtain from the condition (
S.298
)

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{U}_{\tau,\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{U}_{\tau,\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right\|\leq|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|Q(\tau,s)\leq|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\check{Q}(\tau,s).

(S.407)

Therefore, for
[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]
in (
S.406
), we can derive a similar inequality to (
S.402
):

\displaystyle\left\|\left[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{s=0}^{\infty}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\cdot\bar{J}_{r-s}\check{Q}(\tau,s)\leq 4\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.408)

where
\bar{J}_{r-s}=\bar{J}_{0}
for
r-s\leq 0
, and we use the same inequality as (
S.403
) in the second inequality.

We then consider the first term
4\mathcal{N}_{O}\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|\check{Q}(\tau,r)
in (
S.406
), which necessitates the estimation of
\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|
.
To estimate it, we use Lemma
2
.
Because of
{\rm tr}\left([U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right)=0
, the inequality (
S.36
) gives

\displaystyle\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in\Lambda}\sup_{u_{i}}\left\|[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]\right\|=\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i:d_{i,\mathfrak{L}}=r}\sup_{u_{i}}\left\|[[U_{\tau},H_{0}],u_{i}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[r])|\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.409)

where we use the upper bounds of (
S.408
) in the second inequality and
|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[r])|\leq|\mathfrak{L}|\gamma r^{D-1}
in the third inequality.
By applying the inequalities (
S.408
) and (
S.409
) to (
S.406
), we have

[
3rd term in (
S.393
)
]

\displaystyle=\left\|\left[[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 16\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)+8\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r).

(S.410)

Finally, we combine all the upper bounds (
S.396
), (
S.405
),
(
S.410
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(O),u_{i}]\right\|\leq\left\|[[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}]\right\|+\left\|[[H_{0},O],u_{i}\right\|+\left\|[[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O],u_{i}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\left(6\left\|V\right\|\tau\check{Q}(\tau,r)+\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)+4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)+2\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\left[\left(6\left\|V\right\|\tau+4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\right)\check{Q}(\tau,r)+3\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathcal{N}_{O}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\left(6\left\|V\right\|\tau+4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\cdot 2^{2D+5}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0}+3\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\cdot 2^{2D+2}\left(\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{2D}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}\check{Q}(\tau,r)\left[24\left\|V\right\|\tau+2^{2D+6}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\bar{J}_{0}\left(\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{2D}+8\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\right)\right],

(S.411)

where we use Lemmas
28
and
29
in the third inequality, i.e.,

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 2^{2D+2}\left(\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{2D}\right)\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,r),\quad\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 2^{2D+5}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0}.

(S.412)

Thus, we prove the first main inequality (
S.333
) with the choice of
g_{\tau,r}
as in Eq. (
S.334
).

The second main inequality (
S.335
) is derived in the same way.
From Eq. (
S.392
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(O)\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|[U_{\tau}\hat{V}_{\tau}U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]+[H_{0},O]+[[U_{\tau},H_{0}]U_{\tau}^{\dagger},O]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|O\right\|+\left\|H_{0},O\right\|+\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|,

(S.413)

where we use from Eq. (
S.295
)

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{V}_{\tau}\right\|\leq 2\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|U_{\tau_{1}}^{\dagger}VU_{\tau_{1}}\right\|d\tau_{1}\leq 2\tau\left\|V\right\|.

(S.414)

For the estimation of
\left\|H_{0},O\right\|
, using the decomposition of Eq. (
S.397
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|[H_{0},O]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}\neq\emptyset}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\cdot\left\|\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}}\right\|+2\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}[s]\neq\emptyset}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\cdot\left\|\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s]}-\tilde{O}_{\mathfrak{L}[s-1]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathcal{N}_{O}\sum_{s=0}^{\infty}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|\cdot|\partial(\mathfrak{L}[s])|\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,s),

(S.415)

where we use
\sum_{Z:Z\cap\mathfrak{L}[s]\neq\emptyset}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\leq\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[s]}\sum_{Z:Z\ni i}\left\|h_{Z}\right\|\leq\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[s]}\bar{J}_{0}=\bar{J}_{0}|\mathfrak{L}[s]|
from Eq. (
S.6
).
The RHS of the above inequality is equal to the half of the RHS of (
S.402
) with
r=0
, and hence the inequality (
S.405
) with
r=0
gives

\displaystyle\left\|[H_{0},O]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\tilde{Q}(\tau,0)\leq 2^{2D+4}\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0},

(S.416)

where we use
\tilde{Q}(\tau,0)\leq 2^{2D+2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\check{Q}(\tau,0)\leq 2^{2D+3}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}
from Lemma
28
and
\check{Q}(\tau,0)\leq 2
.
Also, the norm of
\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|
has been upper-bounded by the inequality (
S.409
).
Then, Lemma
29
gives

\displaystyle\left\|[U_{\tau},H_{0}]\right\|\leq 4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\sum_{r=0}^{\infty}r^{D-1}\tilde{Q}(\tau,r)\leq 4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\cdot 2^{2D+4}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0}.

(S.417)

By applying the inequalities (
S.416
) and (
S.417
) to (
S.413
), we prove the inequality (
S.335
) as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|O\right\|+2^{2D+5}\mathcal{N}_{O}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}+4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\cdot 2^{2D+4}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0}\left\|O\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}\left[2\tau\left\|V\right\|+2^{2D+5}\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\check{C}_{2,\tau}^{2D}\bar{J}_{0}\left(1+2\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|\check{C}_{3,\tau}^{3D}\right)\right],

(S.418)

where we use
\left\|O\right\|\leq\mathcal{N}_{O}
.
This completes the proof of Proposition
24
.
\square

S.V.4
Proof of Proposition
25

We begin with estimating the first term in (
S.341
), i.e.,

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}dtg_{1}(t)\int_{0}^{t}dt_{1}\left\|\left[O,u_{i}(H_{0},-t_{1})\right]\right\|=\int_{|t|>\delta t}dt|g_{\beta}(t)|\int_{0}^{t}dt_{1}\left\|\left[O,u_{i}(H_{0},-t_{1})\right]\right\|.

(S.419)

For the proof, from Corollary
6
,
we utilize Lemma
5
with
f(t)=|tg_{1}(t)|
, where
g_{1}(t)
has been defined as in Eq. (
S.323
).
Therefore, the parameters
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
in (
S.59
) are upper-bounded using (
S.358
) in Lemma
26
as follows:

\displaystyle f_{1}=\int_{|t|>\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\zeta(2)=\frac{\beta^{2}}{12},

\displaystyle f_{2}=\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{2}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta^{3}\zeta(3)}{2\pi^{3}}\leq\frac{\beta^{3}}{\pi^{3}},

(S.420)

where we use
\zeta(3)\approx 1.20206
in the second inequality.
Also,
f_{t_{0}}(s)
is given by using Eq. (
S.361
)

\displaystyle f_{t_{0}}(s)=\int_{|t|>t_{0}}|tg_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\left[-\frac{2\pi t_{0}}{\beta}\log\left(1-e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}\right)+L_{2}\left(e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\cdot\frac{2\pi t_{0}}{\beta}\log\left(e+\frac{e\beta}{2\pi t_{0}}\right)e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\beta\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}{\pi}(s-2)e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell(s-2)}\quad\textrm{for}\quad s\geq 3,

(S.421)

where we use the same analysis as in (
S.364
) for
-\log\left(1-e^{-2\pi t_{0}/\beta}\right)
,
L_{2}(x)\leq\pi^{2}x/6
for
0\leq x\leq 1
,
and
2\pi t_{0}/\beta\geq\pi\mu(s-2)\Delta\ell/(v\beta)\geq 2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell(s-2)
.
Also,
L_{s}(x)
is the polylogarithm, i.e.,
L_{s}(x):=\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}x^{m}/m^{s}
.
We notice that
x\log(e+e/x)e^{-x}
monotonically decreases with
x
for
x\geq 1
.
For
s\leq 2
, we use
f_{t_{0}}(s)\leq f_{1}
, where we can apply the same inequality as (
S.421
) for
s=2
since
\beta^{2}/12=f_{1}
.

We then estimate the summation in (
S.60
):

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)\left[Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\right]

(S.422)

using the estimations (
S.420
) and (
S.421
).
From the calculation (
S.365
), we obtain

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s\Delta\ell/2}\leq\frac{\beta^{3}}{\pi^{3}}\cdot 4\gamma Cv\left(\frac{8D^{2}}{e\mu}\right)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell).

(S.423)

Also, by employing the same calculation as (
S.366
), we have

\displaystyle 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s\Delta\ell)f_{t_{0}}(s)

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}\mathcal{F}(\ell-\Delta\ell)+2\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell)\sum_{s=2}^{\infty}s^{D}e^{\kappa_{\beta}s\Delta\ell}\left(\frac{2\beta\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}{\pi}(s-2)+\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}\right)e^{-2\kappa_{\beta}(s-2)\Delta\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta^{2}}{6}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+2\gamma(2\Delta\ell)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell)e^{3\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\sum_{\tilde{s}=1}^{\infty}\tilde{s}^{D}\left(\frac{2\beta\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}{\pi}\tilde{s}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}\right)e^{-\kappa_{\beta}\tilde{s}\Delta\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta^{2}}{6}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+2\gamma(2\Delta\ell)^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell)e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\left[\frac{2\beta\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}{\pi}\left(1+2^{D+1}(D+1)!\right)+\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}(1+2^{D}D!)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma(\Delta\ell)^{D}\beta^{2}}{6}e^{\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\mathcal{F}(\ell)+2\gamma(4D\Delta\ell)^{D}e^{2\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}\left(\frac{16D\beta\kappa_{\beta}\Delta\ell}{\pi}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{6}\right)\mathcal{F}(\ell),

(S.424)

where we use
\left(1+2^{D+1}(D+1)!\right)/(1+2^{D}D!)\leq 2(D+1)\leq 4D
and
1+2^{D}D!\leq 2(2D)^{D}
.
Therefore, by applying the inequalities (
S.420
) and
(
S.424
) to (
S.58
), we prove the first main inequality (
S.341
) by choosing
\mathfrak{g}_{3}
as in Eq. (
S.342
).

We next estimate the second term in Eq. (
S.341
).
In this case, as in the proof of Proposition
23
, we simply choose
\Delta\ell=1
, where we can apply the inequality (
S.60
) in Lemma
5
.
To apply Corollary
6
with
f(t)=|tg_{2}(t)|
[see Eq. (
S.323
)], we estimate the parameter
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle f_{1}=\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|t^{2}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta\delta t^{2}}{2\pi},\quad f_{2}=\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|t^{3}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta\delta t^{3}}{3\pi},

(S.425)

where we apply the inequality (
S.359
) with
m=1
and
m=2
, respectively.
Then, by following the same analyses as the inequalities (
S.369
) and (
S.370
), we obtain

\displaystyle Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\leq\frac{Cv\beta\delta t^{3}}{3\pi}e^{-\mu s/2}.

(S.426)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}s^{D}\mathcal{F}(\ell-s)\left[Cf_{2}ve^{-\mu s/2}+f_{t_{0}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\beta\delta t^{2}}{3\pi}\cdot 2^{D}D!Cv(1+4/\mu)^{D}\delta t\mathcal{F}(\ell),

(S.427)

which yields the second inequality (
S.341
) from (
S.58
) with the parameter
\mathfrak{g}_{4}
in Eq. (
S.342
).
This completes the proof of Proposition
25
.
\square

S.VI
Refined locality estimation for entanglement Hamiltonian

In discussing the conditional mutual information, it is not enough to ensure only the quasi-locality of the entanglement Hamiltonian as in Subtheorem
1
.
To see the point, we consider the following entanglement Hamiltonian,

\displaystyle\beta\hat{H}_{\tau}=\log\left(e^{\tau(V_{A}+V_{B})}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau(V_{A}+V_{B})}\right),

(S.428)

where
V_{A}
and
V_{B}
are supported on the subsets
A
and
B
, respectively.
Here, the problem is whether or not we can ensure

\displaystyle\beta\hat{H}_{\tau}\overset{\text{?}}{\approx}\log\left(e^{\tau V_{A}}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V_{A}}\right)+\log\left(e^{\tau V_{B}}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V_{B}}\right)-\beta H_{0},

(S.429)

if
A
and
B
are separated by a sufficiently long distance.
Based on the above approximation, we can estimate the operators
H_{\tilde{\rho}_{\beta}}(A:C|B)
in Lemma
14
and
H_{\rho_{\beta},\tau}(A:C|B)
in Corollary
17
, which appear in the upper bounds for the conditional mutual information.
In Sec.
S.VIII
, we utilize this kind of approximation in completing the proof of the clustering for the conditional mutual information.

Figure 11:
Schematic picture to describe Theorem
2
.
We consider the connection of the exponential operators as
e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V}=e^{\beta\hat{H}_{\tau}}
.
Then, Subtheorem
1
implies that the effective interaction terms, i.e.,
\hat{H}_{\tau}-H_{0}
, are localized around the region
\mathfrak{L}
. However, it does not mean that the effective terms can be determined only by the subset Hamiltonian
H_{0,\mathfrak{L}[r]}
. The purpose of this theorem is to ensure this point; that is, the effective terms can be approximately determined only by using the information in the region
\mathfrak{L}[r]=\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}
, which is given by the inequality (
S.437
).

For the present purpose, we prove the following theorem (see Fig.
11
):

Theorem 2
.

We adopt the same setup as in Subtheorem
1
.
Let
\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}
be an extended subset from
L
by a distance
r
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}:=\mathfrak{L}[r].

(S.430)

Then, we construct
\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}
using the subset Hamiltonian
H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}
on
\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}\subset\Lambda
as follows (see Fig.
11
):

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}=\log\left(e^{\tau V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}e^{\beta H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}e^{\tau V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}\right).

(S.431)

Using Corollary
19
, we can formally express
\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}
as

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}=U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\beta H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger},

\displaystyle U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}:=\mathcal{T}e^{-i\int_{0}^{\tau}{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}d\tau_{1}},\quad\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}:=2\int_{0}^{\tau}U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}d\tau_{1},\quad{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}:=\frac{2}{\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{\beta}(t)V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)dt.

(S.432)

Then, the norm difference between
U_{\tau}
and
U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}
is upper-bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|U_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|\leq\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)=\mathcal{K}_{2}(1+2\tau)e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau+\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2},

(S.433)

where
\mathcal{K}_{0}=\kappa_{0}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}=\kappa_{1}
as in Eq. (
S.299
), and
\mathcal{K}_{2}
are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}:=\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+3},|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+2},r^{2D+3}\right).

(S.434)

Remark.

From the expression of
\kappa_{0}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)
and
\kappa_{1}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|
in Eq. (
S.299
), we can reduce (
S.433
) to the form of

\displaystyle\left\|U_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|\leq e^{\Theta(\tau\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|r\tau)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}.

(S.435)

Using the above inequality, we obtain for
\log\left(e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V}\right)=U_{\tau}(\beta H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau})U_{\tau}^{\dagger}=\hat{H}_{\tau}

\displaystyle\left\|\log\left(e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V}\right)-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\beta H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|\leq\left\|\hat{H}_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\beta H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau})U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|+\left\|\hat{V}_{\tau}-\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\hat{H}_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}\hat{H}_{\tau}U_{\tau}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|+2\left\|\int_{0}^{\tau}\left[U_{\tau_{1}}^{\dagger}VU_{\tau_{1}}-U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right]d\tau_{1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left[\hat{H}_{\tau},U_{\tau}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right]\right\|+2\tau\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|+2\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\|\left[V,U_{\tau_{1}}U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right]\right\|d\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl\{2\tau\left\|V\right\|+4\bar{J}_{0}|\mathfrak{L}|\left[r^{\ast D}+2\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|\cdot 2^{2D+2}\left(C_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{\ast 2D}\right)\right]+4\gamma^{3}|\mathfrak{L}|^{3}\cdot 2^{2D+5}C_{2,\tau}^{2D}C_{3,\tau}^{3D}\bar{J}_{0}\Bigr\}\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{E}(0,r)+4\tau\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r),

(S.436)

where we use the inequality (
S.544
) for
\left\|\left[\hat{H}_{\tau},U_{\tau}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right]\right\|
(
U_{\tau}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}-1=\Delta U_{\tau}
), the inequality (
S.440
) for
\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|
, and the inequality
\left\|\left[V,U_{\tau_{1}}U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right]\right\|\leq 2\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left\|U_{\tau_{1}}-U_{\tau_{1},\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|
.
We note that
r^{\ast}=\Theta(r,\tau)
as is defined in Eq. (
S.478
).
Therefore, the same upper bound as (
S.435
) holds for
\left\|\log\left(e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V}\right)-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|
:

\displaystyle\left\|\log\left(e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0}}e^{\tau V}\right)-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\beta H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|\leq e^{\Theta(\tau\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|r\tau)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}.

(S.437)

Finally, when we consider the entanglement Hamiltonian
\log\left(e^{\tau V}e^{\beta H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}e^{\tau V}\right)
, we can obtain the same inequality as (
S.433
), but in this case, the entanglement Hamiltonian is not strictly defined on
\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}
.

S.VI.1
Proof of Theorem
2

First of all, we upper-bound
\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|
using Lemma
2
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}^{\rm c}}\sup_{u_{i}}\left\|[V,u_{i}]\right\|\leq\left\|V\right\|\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[r]^{\rm c}}Q(0,d_{i,\mathfrak{L}})\leq\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[r]^{\rm c}}\left\|V\right\|e^{-\kappa_{\beta}d_{i,\mathfrak{L}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|V\right\|\cdot\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|e^{\kappa_{\beta}/2}(2/\kappa_{\beta})^{D}D!e^{-r\kappa_{\beta}/2},

(S.438)

where we use the condition (
S.296
), and in the last inequality, we calculate as

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathfrak{L}[r]^{\rm c}}Q(0,d_{i,\mathfrak{L}})\leq\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{L}}\sum_{i:d_{i,j}>r}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}d_{i,j}}\leq\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|e^{\kappa_{\beta}/2}(2/\kappa_{\beta})^{D}D!e^{-r\kappa_{\beta}/2}

(S.439)

from the inequality (
S.21
).
Therefore, choosing
\mathcal{K}_{2}
such that
\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|e^{\kappa_{\beta}/2}(2/\kappa_{\beta})^{D}D!\leq\mathcal{K}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|V\right\|\cdot\mathcal{K}_{2}e^{-r\kappa_{\beta}/2}=\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{E}(0,r).

(S.440)

We next consider the proof of the inequality (
S.433
).
As in the proof of Subtheorem
1
, we rely on the inductive method.
For
\tau=0
, the inequality trivially holds since
U_{\tau}=U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}=\hat{1}
, and we assume the main inequality (
S.433
) up to a certain
\tau
and prove the case of
\tau+d\tau
with
d\tau\to+0
.
Then, using Eq. (
S.295
) as

\displaystyle U_{\tau}=\mathcal{T}e^{-i\int_{0}^{\tau}{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau_{1}}d\tau_{1}},

(S.441)

we have

\displaystyle\left\|U_{\tau+d\tau}-U_{\tau+d\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e^{-i{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}d\tau}U_{\tau}-e^{-i{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}d\tau}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(e^{-i{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}d\tau}-e^{-i{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}d\tau}\right)U_{\tau}+e^{-i{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}d\tau}\left(U_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}-{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|d\tau+\left\|U_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|.

(S.442)

Therefore, following a similar discussion to the derivation of (
S.306
), we aim to prove the upper bound of

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}-{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{2}{\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{\beta}(t)\left[V\left(\hat{H}_{\tau},t\right)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\left(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t\right)\right]dt\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathcal{K}_{0}+\mathcal{K}_{1}\log(r+\tau+e)\right]\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+2\mathcal{K}_{2}e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau+\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}.

(S.443)

To achieve the above bound, it is enough to prove

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}-{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|\leq\left[\mathcal{K}_{0}+\mathcal{K}_{1}\log(r+\tau+e)\right]\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+\mathcal{K}_{2}\left[Q(\tau,r/2)+\mathcal{E}(0,r)\right],

(S.444)

which implies the inequality (
S.443
) because of

\displaystyle e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau+\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\geq Q(\tau,r/2)=e^{\kappa_{0}\tau+\kappa_{1}\tau\log(r/2+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau+\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\geq\mathcal{E}(0,r)=\mathcal{K}_{2}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2},

(S.445)

for
\tau\geq 0
, where we use
\mathcal{K}_{0}=\kappa_{0}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}=\kappa_{1}
from the definition.

For the analyses of
\left\|{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}-{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|
, we start from the equation of

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{\beta}(t)\left[V\left(\hat{H}_{\tau},t\right)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\left(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t\right)\right]dt=\int_{|t|>\delta t}g_{\beta}(t)\left[V\left(\hat{H}_{\tau},t\right)dt-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\left(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t\right)\right]dt

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+i\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}tg_{\beta}(t)\int_{0}^{1}\left\{\left[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)\right]\left(\hat{H}_{\tau},\lambda t\right)-\left[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}\left(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\right]\left(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},\lambda t\right)\right\}d\lambda dt,

(S.446)

by using Eq. (
S.310
).
We start from the equation (
S.313
), which gives for arbitrary operators
O
and
\tilde{O}

\displaystyle\left\|O(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)-\tilde{O}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|U_{\tau}e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\tilde{O}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|O-\tilde{O}\right\|+4\left\|O\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}\right\|,

(S.447)

where, in the inequality, we use

\displaystyle\left\|U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}^{\dagger}-U_{\tau}e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|U_{\tau}-U_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|\leq 4\left\|O\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r).

(S.448)

In applying the inequality (
S.447
) to
V(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\left(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t\right)
in Eq. (
S.446
), the non-trivial analyses mainly arise from the estimation of the norm difference between
e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}
and

e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}
.
For this purpose, we prove the following proposition (see Sec.
S.VI.2
for the proof):

Proposition 30
.

Let
O
be an arbitrary quasi-local operator around
L
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\left[O,u_{i}\right]\right\|\leq\left\|O\right\|\check{Q}(\tau,r),\quad\check{Q}(\tau,r):=e^{\check{\kappa}_{0}\tau+\check{\kappa}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r},

(S.449)

and

\displaystyle Q(\tau,r)\leq\check{Q}(\tau,r),

(S.450)

where
Q(\tau,r)
is defined by (
S.298
) in Subtheorem
1
,
d_{i,\mathfrak{L}}=r
, and
u_{i}
is an arbitrary local unitary operator on the site
i
.
We then obtain for the third term in the RHS of (
S.447
)

\displaystyle\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 6\gamma\left\|O\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)\Bigl\{2^{D+3}\left(\check{C}_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)\check{Q}(\tau,r/2)+\tilde{J}_{0}|t|r^{D}\left[2e^{-\mu r/8}+\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)\right]\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\quad+4|t|\cdot\left\|O\right\|\cdot\left\|V\right\|\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+2\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathcal{E}(\tau_{1},r)d\tau_{1}\right),

(S.451)

where the parameters
\tilde{J}_{0}
and
\check{C}_{1,\tau}
are defined in Eqs. (
S.17
) and (
S.379
), respectively.

Remark.
Using
\mathcal{E}(\tau_{1},r)=\mathcal{K}_{2}(1+2\tau_{1})e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau+\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau_{1}\log(r+\tau_{1}+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\mathcal{E}(\tau_{1},r)d\tau_{1}\leq\mathcal{K}_{2}(1+2\tau)e^{\mathcal{K}_{1}\tau\log(r+\tau+e)-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{\mathcal{K}_{0}\tau_{1}}d\tau_{1}\leq\frac{\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}},

(S.452)

which upper-bounds the last term in the RHS of (
S.451
).

We estimate the first term in the RHS of (
S.446
) by considering the case of
O=V
and
\tilde{O}=V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}
in (
S.447
).
By applying the inequality (
S.451
) to (
S.447
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|V(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)\Bigl\{2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)Q(0,r/2)+\tilde{J}_{0}|t|r^{D}\left[2e^{-\mu r/8}+\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)\right]\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|+4\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+4|t|\cdot\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right).

(S.453)

Using Lemma
26
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{2\beta}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right),

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta^{2}}{12}\leq\frac{\beta^{2}}{\pi^{2}},\quad\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{2}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\zeta(3)\beta^{3}}{2\pi^{3}}\leq\frac{\beta^{3}}{\pi^{3}}.

(S.454)

We therefore obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|\left[\left\|V-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|+4\left\|V\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+4|t|\cdot\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)\right]dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)\left[\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+4\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\right]+\frac{4\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|^{2}}{\pi^{2}}\left[\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right],

(S.455)

and

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|\cdot 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)\Bigl\{2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)+2\tilde{J}_{0}|t|r^{D}e^{-\mu r/8}\Bigr\}dt

\displaystyle\leq 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left[2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)\cdot\frac{2\beta}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)+2\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}}{\pi^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+24\tau\gamma\left\|V\right\|^{2}\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left[2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}}{\pi^{2}}+2\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\cdot\frac{\beta^{3}}{\pi^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12\cdot 2^{D+3}\gamma\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|}{\pi}\left(\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)+\frac{2\tau\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\right)\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{12\gamma\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}}{\pi^{2}}\left(\frac{4\beta\left\|V\right\|\tau}{\pi}+1\right)r^{D}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\leq\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}Q(\tau,r/2),

(S.456)

with

\displaystyle\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}

\displaystyle:=\frac{12\cdot 2^{D+3}\gamma\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|}{\pi}\left(\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)+\frac{2\tau\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\right)\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)+\frac{12\gamma\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}}{\pi^{2}}\left(\frac{4\beta\left\|V\right\|\tau}{\pi}+1\right)r^{D}

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+2},|\mathfrak{L}|^{2},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+1},r^{D},\log(\delta t^{-1})\right),

(S.457)

where we use the inequality (
S.440
) and
\mu/8\geq\kappa_{\beta}/2
from the definition (
S.121
), and in the last inequality, we use from Eq. (
S.379
)

\displaystyle C^{D}_{1,\tau}=\left[\frac{\tau+e}{2}+\frac{32}{\kappa_{\beta}^{2}}\left(D^{2}+\kappa_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{\beta}\kappa_{0}\tau}{8}\right)\right]^{D}\leq\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D},|\mathfrak{L}|,\beta^{2D^{2}+2D},\tau^{2D}\right),

(S.458)

where we use
\kappa_{0}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)
and
\kappa_{1}=\Theta(\beta^{D})\left\|V\right\|
from Eq. (
S.300
).
The remaining part in Eq. (
S.453
) gives the integral of

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|\cdot 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)\tilde{J}_{0}|t|r^{D}\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)\Bigr\}dt,

(S.459)

which necessitates the estimation of

\displaystyle 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)|g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)dt.

(S.460)

For this purpose, we prove the following lemma:

Lemma 31
.

For an arbitrary
m\in\mathbb{N}
such that
m\geq 1
, we obtain the upper bound of

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left[C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right]dt\leq 6e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{m}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3m}+m!\right).

(S.461)

Proof of Lemma
31
.

We first let
t_{0}=\mu r/(8v)
and obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left[C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right]dt

\displaystyle\leq\int_{|t|\leq t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left[C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right]dt+\int_{|t|>t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left[C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right]dt

\displaystyle\leq C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{vt_{0}-\mu r/4}\int_{|t|\leq t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt+\int_{|t|>t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt.

(S.462)

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{vt_{0}-\mu r/4}\int_{|t|\leq t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{-\mu r/8}\cdot\frac{2}{m}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}\right)^{m},

(S.463)

where, in the first inequality, we use the inequality (
S.362
) in the proof of Lemma
26
.

For the second term, by letting
z=2\pi t/\beta
and
z_{0}=2\pi t_{0}/\beta=\pi\mu r/(4v\beta)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>t_{0}}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle=2\int_{z_{0}}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\beta z}{2\pi}\right)^{m}\frac{e^{-z}}{1-e^{-z}}\frac{\beta dz}{2\pi}=2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\int_{z_{0}}^{\infty}\frac{z^{m}}{e^{z}-1}dz.

(S.464)

For the integral, we can derive

\displaystyle\int_{z_{0}}^{\infty}\frac{z^{m}}{e^{z}-1}dz

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{m}\frac{m!}{(m-s)!}z_{0}^{m-s}{\rm Li}_{s+1}(e^{-z_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq e^{-z_{0}}\left[(z_{0}+1)^{m}+\sum_{s=1}^{m}\frac{m!}{(m-s)!}z_{0}^{m-s}\zeta(s+1)\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-z_{0}}\left[(z_{0}+1)^{m}+\zeta(2)\sum_{s=1}^{m}s!\binom{m}{s}z_{0}^{m-s}\right]\leq 3e^{-z_{0}}m!(z_{0}+1)^{m},

(S.465)

where
{\rm Li}_{s}(x)
is the polylogarithm function, i.e.,
{\rm Li}_{s}(x):=\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}x^{k}/k^{s}
[
{\rm Li}_{1}(x)=-\log(1-x)
], and we use the inequality of

\displaystyle z_{0}^{m}{\rm Li}_{1}(e^{-z_{0}})=-z_{0}^{m}\log(1-e^{-z_{0}})\leq(z_{0}+1)^{m}e^{-z_{0}}

(S.466)

and

\displaystyle{\rm Li}_{s}(e^{-z_{0}})=\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-kz_{0}}}{k^{s}}=e^{-z_{0}}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-(k-1)z_{0}}}{k^{s}}\leq e^{-z_{0}}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{k^{s}}=e^{-z_{0}}\zeta(s)\quad\textrm{for}\quad s\geq 2.

(S.467)

We thus obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>t_{0}}|g_{\beta}(t)|dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\cdot 3e^{-\pi\mu r/(4v\beta)}m!\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{m}.

(S.468)

By combining the inequalities (
S.463
) and (
S.468
) with (
S.462
), we prove the main inequality (
S.461
) as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t^{m}g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left[C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right]dt

\displaystyle\leq C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{-\mu r/8}\cdot\frac{2}{m}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}\right)^{m}+2\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\cdot 3e^{-\pi\mu r/(4v\beta)}m!\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{m}

\displaystyle\leq 6e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{m+1}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{m}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3m}+m!\right),

(S.469)

where we use the definition (
S.121
) for
\kappa_{\beta}
.
This completes the proof.
\square

[ End of Proof of Lemma
31
]

Using this lemma, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)|g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq 6e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\left\{4\tau\left\|V\right\|\cdot\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{6}+2\right)+\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3}+1\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\beta^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\left(\frac{4\tau\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}+1\right)\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3}+2\right)=\Theta(\left\|V\right\|,|\mathfrak{L}|,\beta^{3},\tau,r^{D+2})e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2},

(S.470)

which upper-bound the term (
S.460
) as

\displaystyle 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}\int_{|t|>\delta t}|t|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)|g_{\beta}(t)|\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\gamma\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}}{2\pi^{2}}\left(\frac{4\tau\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}+1\right)\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3}+2\right)=\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}Q(\tau,r/2)

(S.471)

with

\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}

\displaystyle:=\frac{18\gamma\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}}{2\pi^{2}}\left(\frac{4\tau\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}+1\right)\left(\frac{\pi\mu r}{4v\beta}+1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}}{3}+2\right)Q(\tau,r/2)

\displaystyle\leq\Theta(\left\|V\right\|^{2},|\mathfrak{L}|^{2},\beta^{3},\tau,r^{2D+2}).

(S.472)

By combining the three upper bounds (
S.455
), (
S.456
) and (
S.471
) with the integral of (
S.453
), we upper-bound the first term in the RHS of (
S.446
) as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{|t|>\delta t}|g_{\beta}(t)|\cdot\left\|V(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)dt-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)\right\|dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+4\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\right)+\frac{4\beta^{2}\left\|V\right\|^{2}}{\pi^{2}}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)+\left(\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}+\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)

\displaystyle\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}+\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}\leq\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+2},|\mathfrak{L}|^{2},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+1},r^{2D+2},\log(\delta t^{-1})\right).

(S.473)

We next estimate the second term in the RHS of (
S.446
) by considering the case of
O={\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)
and
\tilde{O}={\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})
in (
S.447
).
Then, we have to estimate

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|\int_{0}^{1}\left\|[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)](\hat{H}_{\tau},\lambda t)-[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})](\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},\lambda t)\right\|d\lambda dt.

(S.474)

Using the inequality (
S.447
) yields

\displaystyle\left\|[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)](\hat{H}_{\tau},\lambda t)-[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})](\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},\lambda t)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)-{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})\right\|+4\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}\right\|.

(S.475)

From the proposition
24
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V),u_{i}\right]\right\|\leq\left\|V\right\|g_{\tau,r}Q(\tau,r),

(S.476)

and

\displaystyle\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)\right\|\leq\left\|V\right\|g^{\prime}_{\tau},

(S.477)

where the parameters
g_{\tau,r}
and
g^{\prime}_{\tau}
are estimated in Eq. (
S.337
).
Furthermore, we need to estimate the norm difference between
{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)
and
{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})
.
For this purpose, we prove the following statement (see Sec.
S.VI.3
for the proof):

Proposition 32
.

Let us define
r^{\ast}
be the minimum number such that

\displaystyle\min\left[\mathcal{E}(\tau,r),Q(\tau,r^{\ast})\right]=Q(\tau,r^{\ast})\quad\textrm{i.e.,}\quad r^{\ast}=\Theta(\tau,r).

(S.478)

We upper-bound the norm of
\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)-{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})\right\|
by

\displaystyle\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)-{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})\right\|\leq\left\|V\right\|g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}\mathcal{E}(\tau,r),

(S.479)

where we define
g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}
as

\displaystyle g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}:=6\left[3\tau\left\|V\right\|+\tilde{J}_{0}|\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}|^{2}+\bar{J}_{0}|\mathfrak{L}|r^{\ast D}+2^{2D+3}\bar{J}_{0}\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|^{2}\left(C_{2,\tau}^{2D}+r^{\ast 2D}+4\gamma^{2}|\mathfrak{L}|C_{2,\tau}^{2D}C_{3,\tau}^{3D}\right)\right].

(S.480)

Remark.
Using the
\Theta
notation, we obtain

\displaystyle g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}=\Theta(\left\|V\right\|,|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},r^{2D},\beta^{10D},\kappa_{0}^{5D},\kappa_{1}^{10D},\tau^{10D}).

(S.481)

We again remind readers that for our purpose it is enough to ensure that
g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}
is upper-bounded by a polynomial of
\{\left\|V\right\|,|\mathfrak{L}|,r,\beta,\kappa_{0},\kappa_{1},\tau
}.

From Lemma
26
, we can derive

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|t^{m+1}g_{\beta}(t)|dt\leq\frac{\beta}{(m+1)\pi}\delta t^{m+1}\leq\frac{\beta}{\pi}\delta t^{m+1}\quad(m\geq 0).

(S.482)

Applying the above inequality to (
S.474
) and (
S.475
), we first obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|\int_{0}^{1}\left(\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)-{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})\right\|+4\left\|{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)\right\|\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\right)d\lambda dt\leq\frac{\beta\delta t\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\left(g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}+4g^{\prime}_{\tau}\right).

(S.483)

Next, by using the inequality (
S.451
) with the bounds of (
S.476
) and (
S.477
), we upper-bound

\displaystyle\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 6\gamma\left\|V\right\|g^{\prime}_{\tau}|\mathfrak{L}|\left(4|t|\tau\left\|V\right\|+1\right)\Bigl\{2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)g_{\tau,r}Q(\tau,r/2)+\tilde{J}_{0}|t|r^{D}\left[2e^{-\mu r/8}+\min\left(C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v|t|-\mu r/4},1\right)\right]\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\quad+4|t|g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right),

(S.484)

where
0\leq\lambda\leq 1
, and the quasi-locality of
{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)
is given by Proposition
24
.
The integral of the above upper bounds reads

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|\int_{0}^{1}\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)\lambda t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)U_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)\lambda t}\right\|d\lambda dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\beta\gamma\left\|V\right\|g^{\prime}_{\tau}|\mathfrak{L}|\delta t}{\pi}\left(4\left\|V\right\|\tau\delta t+1\right)\Bigl[2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)g_{\tau,r}Q(\tau,r/2)+\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}\delta t\left(2e^{-\mu r/8}+C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v\delta t-\mu r/4}\right)\Bigr]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\beta\delta t^{2}}{\pi}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}\delta t\left\|V\right\|Q(\tau,r/2)+\frac{4\beta\delta t^{2}}{\pi}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right),

(S.485)

where, in the last inequality, the parameter
\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}
is defined as

\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}=\frac{6\beta\gamma g^{\prime}_{\tau}|\mathfrak{L}|}{\pi}\left(4\left\|V\right\|\tau\delta t+1\right)\Bigl[2^{D+3}\left(C_{1,\tau}^{D}+r^{D}\right)g_{\tau,r}+\tilde{J}_{0}r^{D}\delta t\left(2+C\gamma|\mathfrak{L}|r^{D}e^{v\delta t-\mu r/8}\right)\Bigr],

(S.486)

and we use
e^{-\mu r/8}\leq e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\leq Q(\tau,r/2)
and
e^{-\mu r/4}\leq e^{-\mu r/8}e^{-\kappa_{\beta}r/2}\leq e^{-\mu r/8}Q(\tau,r/2)
.
Note that by using the inequality (
S.337
), i.e.,

\displaystyle g_{\tau,r}\leq\Theta(\left\|V\right\|,|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},r^{2D},\beta^{10D},\kappa_{0}^{5D},\kappa_{1}^{10D},\tau^{10D}),

\displaystyle g^{\prime}_{\tau}\leq\Theta(\left\|V\right\|,|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},\beta^{10D},\kappa_{0}^{5D},\kappa_{1}^{10D},\tau^{10D}),

(S.487)

we have

\displaystyle\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}\leq\Theta(\left\|V\right\|^{3},|\mathfrak{L}|^{7},r^{3D},\beta^{22D+1},\kappa_{0}^{11D},\kappa_{1}^{22D},\tau^{22D+1}).

(S.488)

Therefore, combining the inequalities (
S.483
) and (
S.485
) with (
S.474
), we reach the desired inequality of

\displaystyle\int_{|t|\leq\delta t}|tg_{\beta}(t)|\int_{0}^{1}\left\|[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau}}(V)](\hat{H}_{\tau},\lambda t)-[{\rm ad}_{\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}}(V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}})](\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},\lambda t)\right\|d\lambda dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta\delta t\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\left(g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}+3g^{\prime}_{\tau}\right)+\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}\delta t\left\|V\right\|Q(\tau,r/2)+\frac{4\beta\delta t^{2}}{\pi}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right).

(S.489)

Finally, by applying the inequalities (
S.473
) and (
S.489
) to (
S.446
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{2}{\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{\beta}(t)\left[V(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)\right]dt\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+4\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\right)+\frac{8\beta\left\|V\right\|^{2}}{\pi^{2}}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)+\frac{2}{\beta}\left(\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}+\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\delta t\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)\left(g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}+3g^{\prime}_{\tau}\right)+\frac{2}{\beta}\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}\delta t\left\|V\right\|Q(\tau,r/2)+\frac{8\delta t^{2}}{\pi}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|^{2}\left(\tau\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\left[8\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)+\frac{8\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi\mathcal{K}_{0}}+\delta t\left(g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}+3g^{\prime}_{\tau}\right)+\frac{8\delta t^{2}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right]\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\tau\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta}{2\pi\delta t}\right)+\frac{2\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\pi}+2\delta t^{2}g^{\prime}_{\tau}\left\|V\right\|\right]\mathcal{E}(0,r)+\frac{2}{\beta}\left(\zeta_{\tau,r,\delta t}+\zeta^{\prime}_{\tau,r}+\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}\delta t\left\|V\right\|\right)Q(\tau,r/2).

(S.490)

We thus obtain the upper bound of
\left\|{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau}-{\mathcal{C}}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right\|
, which is equal to the LHS of the above inequality as in Eq. (
S.443
).

S.VI.1.1
Completing the proof of Theorem
2

For the proof, we choose
\delta t
such that

\displaystyle\delta t\leq\frac{1}{\left\|V\right\|}\min\left(\frac{1}{g^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r}+3g^{\prime}_{\tau}},\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime\prime}_{\tau,r,\delta t}}\right),

(S.491)

which reduces the inequality (
S.490
) to

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{2}{\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g_{\beta}(t)\left[V(\hat{H}_{\tau},t)-V_{\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}(\hat{H}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}},t)\right]dt\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\left[\log(\beta/\delta t)+\frac{\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right]\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\left[\log(\beta/\delta t)+\beta\left\|V\right\|\right]\mathcal{E}(0,r)

\displaystyle+\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+2},|\mathfrak{L}|^{2},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+1},\tau^{2D+1},r^{2D+2},\log(\delta t^{-1})\right)Q(\tau,r/2).

(S.492)

Here,
\log(1/\delta t)
is given by

\displaystyle\log(\beta/\delta t)\leq\Theta(1)\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1})+\Theta(1)\log(r+\tau+e)\leq\Theta(1)\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\Theta(1)\log(r+\tau+e).

(S.493)

We note that
\kappa_{0}
and
\kappa_{1}
have been given by Eq. (
S.299
).
Then, in order to satisfy the inequality (
S.444
), we have to choose

\mathcal{K}_{0}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}
so that the following condition is satisfied:

\displaystyle\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\left(\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\log(r+\tau+e)+\frac{\beta\left\|V\right\|}{\mathcal{K}_{0}}\right)\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)

\displaystyle+\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\Bigl[\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\log(r+\tau+e)+\beta\left\|V\right\|\Bigr]\mathcal{E}(0,r)

\displaystyle+\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+3},|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+2},r^{2D+3}\right)Q(\tau,r/2)

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathcal{K}_{0}+\mathcal{K}_{1}\log(r+\tau+e)\right]\mathcal{E}(\tau,r)+\mathcal{K}_{2}\left[Q(\tau,r/2)+\mathcal{E}(0,r)\right].

(S.494)

Thus, the following choices satisfy the above condition.

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{0}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigl[\kappa_{0},\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)\Bigr],\quad\mathcal{K}_{1}=\max\Bigl[\kappa_{1},\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\Bigr],

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}

\displaystyle=\max\left[\Theta(\left\|V\right\|)\left(\log(\beta\left\|V\right\|\cdot|\mathfrak{L}|)+\log(r+\tau+e)+\beta\left\|V\right\|\right),\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+3},|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+2},r^{2D+3}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\left\|V\right\|^{2D+3},|\mathfrak{L}|^{3},\beta^{2D^{2}+2D+2},\tau^{2D+2},r^{2D+3}\right),

(S.495)

where we take
\mathcal{K}_{0}\geq\kappa_{0}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}\geq\kappa_{1}
into account.
By making the
\kappa_{0}
and
\kappa_{1}
sufficiently large, we can make
\mathcal{K}_{0}=\kappa_{0}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}=\kappa_{1}
.
We thus prove the main inequality (
S.433
).
This completes the proof of Theorem
2
.
\square

S.VI.2
Proof of Proposition
30

The purpose here is to estimate the upper bound of the norm difference of

\displaystyle\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}\right\|

(S.496)

for an arbitrary operator
O
.
To estimate it, we first prove the following lemma:

Lemma 33
.

Let us define the operator function
\varepsilon_{\tau,t}(O_{1})
as follows:

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\tau,t}(O_{1}):=\left\|e^{iH_{0}t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}O_{1}U_{\tau}e^{-iH_{0}t}-e^{iH_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}O_{1}U_{\tau}e^{-iH_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}t}\right\|,

(S.497)

where
O_{1}
is an arbitrary operator.
We then obtain

\displaystyle\left\|e^{i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0}+\hat{V}_{\tau}\right)t}-e^{i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}U_{\tau}^{\dagger}OU_{\tau}e^{-i\left(H_{0,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}+\hat{V}_{\tau,\tilde{\mathfrak{L}}}\right)t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq
